	#version 300 es
layout(location = 0) in int aFrame;
layout(location = 1) in float aX;
layout(location = 2) in float aY;
layout(location = 3) in float aRot;
layout(location = 4) in float aSx;
layout(location = 5) in float aSy;
layout(location = 6) in float aR;
layout(location = 7) in float aG;
layout(location = 8) in float aB;
layout(location = 9) in float aA;
layout(std140) uniform SkywardFrames {
    vec4 uRect[768];
    vec4 uFlags[192];
uniform vec4 uView;
uniform vec2 uShake;
uniform float uPxPerTexel;
out vec2 vTexel;
out vec4 vColor;
out float vScale;
flat out int vSmooth;
flat out int vPage;
void main() {
    vec4 rect = uRect[aFrame];
    vec2 corner = vec2(float(gl_VertexID & 1), float(gl_VertexID >> 1));
    vec2 local = (corner - 0.5) * rect.zw * vec2(aSx, aSy);
    float c = cos(aRot);
    float s = sin(aRot);
    vec2 p = vec2(aX, aY) + uShake + vec2(local.x * c - local.y * s, local.x * s + local.y * c);
    gl_Position = vec4(p * uView.xy + uView.zw, 0.0, 1.0);
    vTexel = rect.xy + corner * rect.zw;
    vColor = vec4(vec3(aR, aG, aB) * aA, aA);
    vScale = max(1.0, uPxPerTexel * min(abs(aSx), abs(aSy)));
    int fl = int(uFlags[aFrame >> 2][aFrame & 3] + 0.5);
    vSmooth = fl & 1;
    vPage = fl >> 1;
#version 300 es
precision highp float;
uniform sampler2D uAtlas;
uniform vec2 uAtlasSize;
// Page 1: the Stormpost FX page (bound to the atlas itself when a source has none).
uniform sampler2D uFx;
uniform vec2 uFxSize;
// 1 for BLEND_FILL runs (set per instanced run; a mode change always starts a new run).
uniform int uFill;
in vec2 vTexel;
in vec4 vColor;
in float vScale;
flat in int vSmooth;
flat in int vPage;
out vec4 outColor;
vec2 sharpBilinear(vec2 texel, float scale) {
    vec2 floored = floor(texel);
    vec2 s = fract(texel);
    float regionRange = 0.5 - 0.5 / scale;
    vec2 centerDist = s - 0.5;
    vec2 f = (centerDist - clamp(centerDist, -regionRange, regionRange)) * scale + 0.5;
    return floored + f;
void main() {
    vec2 t = vSmooth == 1 ? vTexel : sharpBilinear(vTexel, vScale);
    vec4 tex = vPage == 1 ? texture(uFx, t / uFxSize) : texture(uAtlas, t / uAtlasSize);
    // vColor is the premultiplied tint (rgb
a, a). Fill: silhouette 
 colour ignored, coverage kept.
    outColor = uFill == 1 ? vColor * tex.a : tex * vColor;
#version 300 es
precision highp float;
uniform sampler2D uTile;
uniform vec2 uTileSize;
uniform float uTileScale;
uniform float uOffsetY;
uniform float uAlpha;
uniform float uPxPerTexel;
// 1 = clamp (sky gradient: edge rows extend), 0 = wrap vertically (scrolling tiles).
uniform int uClamp;
in vec2 vArena;
out vec4 outColor;
vec2 sharpBilinear(vec2 texel, float scale) {
    vec2 floored = floor(texel);
    vec2 s = fract(texel);
    float regionRange = 0.5 - 0.5 / scale;
    vec2 centerDist = s - 0.5;
    vec2 f = (centerDist - clamp(centerDist, -regionRange, regionRange)) * scale + 0.5;
    return floored + f;
void main() {
    vec2 texel = vec2(vArena.x, vArena.y - uOffsetY) / uTileScale;
    texel.y = uClamp == 1 ? clamp(texel.y, 0.5, uTileSize.y - 0.5) : mod(texel.y, uTileSize.y);
    vec2 t = sharpBilinear(texel, max(1.0, uPxPerTexel));
    outColor = texture(uTile, t / uTileSize) * uAlpha;
